Method: 提出基于因果分数的选择器：用归一化activation-patching恢复度对注意力头与MLP块打分，仅在选中位置训练LoRA/QLoRA；其变体额外为层内小子集的联合选择加入有界信用。在Ministral-8B/NF4上设置三个实验单元：Swahili跨度-JSON信息抽取（b=0.25%与1.0%）与英语→Swahili机器翻译（b=1.0%），与随机、幅度、激活范数、梯度/Fisher选择器对比。IE主单元使用3个种子、对600条预测做配对bootstrap置信区间。另做schema与span分解分析。

Result: 因果选择器在所有单元格的主要指标上均未取胜。IE主单元中，因果变体较随机提升+0.079 span+type F1、较梯度/Fisher提升+0.174，但落后activation-norm 0.028，且跨种子标准差最小（±0.003）。MT单元中四种选择器差异均在0.30 BLEU以内，所有配对置信区间包含零。分解分析显示：activation-norm捕获刚性JSON结构流程，而因果分数跟踪内容敏感位置，这解释了IE中的差距来源。

Conclusion: 因果信号可有效引导PEFT放置，但并不优于廉价启发式。作者归纳出初步诊断准则：当输出结构占主导时优先使用activation-norm；在内容主导任务中将因果选择器视为待检验的假设。公开mask、分数、预测与评估文件以支持直接复现。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability identifies sparse subsets of heads and MLP blocks that carry specific behaviors. We ask whether such causal signals can guide where to place a small PEFT budget more effectively than the cheap heuristics practitioners already use. \method{} scores attention heads and MLP blocks by normalized activation-patching recovery on clean/corrupted probes and trains LoRA/QLoRA only on the selected sites; \methodc{} adds bounded credit for small within-layer joint subsets.
  We compare against random, magnitude, activation-norm, and gradient/Fisher on Ministral-8B/NF4 in three cells: Swahili span-JSON information extraction (IE) at $b{=}0.25\%$ and $1.0\%$, and English$\to$Swahili machine translation (MT) at $b{=}1.0\%$. The causal selectors never win the primary metric. On the headline IE cell (3 seeds, paired-bootstrap CIs over $600$ predictions), \methodc{} beats random by $+0.079$ span+type F1 and gradient/Fisher by $+0.174$, but trails activation-norm by $0.028$, with the smallest cross-seed std ($\pm 0.003$). On MT all four selectors lie within $0.30$ BLEU and every paired CI includes zero. A schema-versus-span decomposition explains the IE gap: activation-norm captures the rigid JSON routine, while causal scores track content-sensitive sites. We distill a preliminary diagnostic -- prefer activation-norm when output structure dominates, treat causal selectors as a hypothesis for content-dominated tasks -- and release masks, scores, predictions, and evaluation files for direct replay.

</details>


### [175] [The Death of Schema Linking? Text-to-SQL in the Age of Well-Reasoned Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2408.07702)
*Karime Maamari,Fadhil Abubaker,Daniel Jaroslawicz,Amine Mhedhbi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Text-to-SQL中的schema linking，发现新一代LLM在大量无关schema存在时仍能有效利用相关元素，因此提出在schema可装入上下文窗口时完全放弃schema linking，转而采用增强、选择与纠正等技术，在BIRD基准上以71.83%的准确率排名第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schema linking是Text-to-SQL的关键步骤，但其不完美之处常会误删生成查询所需的必要列，导致准确率下降。

Method: 基于新模型即使面对大量无关schema也能利用相关元素的发现，当schema能放入上下文窗口时直接舍弃schema linking，改用augmentation、selection、correction等上下文处理技术。

Result: 在BIRD基准上取得71.83%的准确率，排名第一。

Conclusion: 在上下文窗口允许时，放弃schema linking并辅以增强、选择、纠正等技术，能有效提升Text-to-SQL准确率。

Abstract: Schema linking is a crucial step in Text-to-SQL pipelines. Its goal is to retrieve the relevant tables and columns of a target database for a user's query while disregarding irrelevant ones. However, imperfect schema linking can often exclude required columns needed for accurate query generation. In this work, we revisit schema linking when using the latest generation of large language models (LLMs). We find empirically that newer models are adept at utilizing relevant schema elements during generation even in the presence of large numbers of irrelevant ones. As such, our Text-to-SQL pipeline entirely forgoes schema linking in cases where the schema fits within the model's context window in order to minimize issues due to filtering required schema elements. Furthermore, instead of filtering contextual information, we highlight techniques such as augmentation, selection, and correction, and adopt them to improve the accuracy of our Text-to-SQL pipeline. Our approach ranks first on the BIRD benchmark achieving an accuracy of 71.83%.

</details>


### [176] [A Benchmark Suite and Ground-Truth Methodology for Formal Verification of IEC 61131-3 Ladder Diagram Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.18994)
*Pierre Dantas,Lucas Cordeiro,Waldir Junior*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 首个面向可编程逻辑控制器（PLC）程序形式化验证的基准套件，同时覆盖文本（ST）与图形（LD）IEC 61131-3 编码，并配套受控真值、形式属性、可机检判定结论与违规见证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具支持不断增长，但领域缺乏标准化评测基准：已有语料缺少形式属性或图形方言，且私有程序集无法支撑可复现的进展度量。同时存在具体失效模式——显而易见的"安全属性"会因不可见的非终止问题，把两个公开逻辑炸弹语料中的所有攻击误判为安全。

Method: 构建包含 50 个程序、83 个变体、覆盖十个工业领域的套件，以 PLCopen XML 与 ST 提供，每个程序配有形式属性、可机检期望判定和 SV-COMP 格式的违规见证。核心方法为三元真值准则：判定结论通过"构造法、故障注入、经审计的跨工具共识"三种途径确立。参考判定由源码构建的 ESBMC v8.4 获得，并用决策过程无关的 nuXmv 在有限状态片段上交叉验证。

Result: ESBMC v8.4 下全部 25 个图形基准可执行，45 个被接受变体中 43 个与记录的判定一致；在有限状态片段（21 个基准）上，nuXmv 对全部 24 个联锁变体判定一致，并解决了两个 ESBMC-PLC 遗留为未知的基准，确认了工具中立的真值与判别力。移植过程暴露出格式与语义碎片化：前端接受的序列化不同，且定时器语义因工具而异。

Conclusion: 该套件为 PLC 程序形式化验证提供了可复现、工具中立且具判别力的评测基准，并揭示了 IEC 61131-3 生态中的格式与语义碎片化问题。语料、模式、验证器与复检工具链以开放制品形式发布。

Abstract: We present the first benchmark suite for formal verification of Programmable Logic Controller (PLC) programs that combines controlled ground truth with coverage of both textual (Structured Text, ST) and graphical (Ladder Diagram, LD) IEC 61131-3 encodings. Despite growing support for tools, the field lacks standard evaluation benchmarks: existing corpora omit formal properties or graphical dialects, and private program sets preclude reproducible measurement of progress. Our suite comprises 50 programs in 83 variants across ten industrial domains, provided in PLCopen Extensible Markup Language (XML) and ST, each paired with a formal property, machine-checkable expected verdict, and violation witness in the Software Verification Competition (SV-COMP) format. The central methodological contribution is a tripartite ground-truth discipline - verdicts are established by construction, fault injection, or audited cross-tool consensus - motivated by a concrete failure mode where the obvious safety property misclassifies all attacks from two public logic-bomb corpora as safe due to invisible non-termination. Reference verdicts are obtained with the Efficient SMT-Based Context-Bounded Model Checker (ESBMC) v8.4 from source: all 25 graphical benchmarks execute, and 43 of 45 accepted variants match recorded verdicts. On the finite-state fragment (21 benchmarks), nuXmv - a model checker with unrelated decision procedures - agrees on all 24 interlock variants and resolves two benchmarks ESBMC-PLC leaves unknown, confirming tool-neutral ground truth and discriminative power. Porting exposes format and semantics fragmentation: front-ends accept different serializations, and timer semantics vary across tools - phenomena the suite is designed to reveal. The corpus, schema, validator, and recheck harness are released as open artifacts.

</details>


### [177] [WordPolo: Evaluating Language Models Through Iterative Semantic Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19006)
*Tyler McDonald,Ali Emami*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 WordPolo，一个通过语义相似度反馈寻找未知目标词的基准任务，用于同时评估大模型推理过程与结果。作者在 1500 个谜题上评测了 LLM、LRM、人类及启发式方法，并引入基于进展的指标揭示仅看准确率无法发现的推理特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 与 LRM 主要在挑战性基准上以数据集准确率进行评价，无法反映其推理过程的质量或忠实性；因此需要一个能够使迭代推理与自适应搜索策略直接可观测、且必须依赖这些能力才能成功的评测范式。

Method: 提出 WordPolo 词语寻找任务：参与者从零知识出发猜测未知目标词，并收到距离分数（1 表示正确，数值越大表示越远）作为语义相似度反馈；成功需解读分数以在语义空间中导航并系统性地缩小搜索范围。在 1500 个谜题上评测了 GPT-4.1、Llama 4、Claude 3.5 Haiku、Qwen 3 等 LLM，o4-mini、Deepseek-R1 等 LRM，人类以及一种新的启发式方法，并引入基于进展（progression-based）的指标。

Result: 求解率范围从 4% 到 62%；基于进展的指标显示模型通常能取得有意义的进展，这是仅凭准确率会忽略的洞见；分析表明推理模型可能受过度思考（overthinking）与思考不足（underthinking）的阻碍，而成功的模型展现出类人策略。

Conclusion: WordPolo 表明需要能够同时测试推理过程与结果的基准，从而对模型能力进行整体性度量；代码与数据集见 https://wordpolo-demo.vercel.app/。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) are typically evaluated on challenging benchmarks through dataset accuracy alone, providing no insight into the quality or faithfulness of their reasoning processes. We present WordPolo, a word-finding task where participants must discover an unknown target word using semantic similarity feedback. Players start with zero knowledge, make guesses, and receive distance scores (1 = correct, higher = further away). Success requires interpreting scores to navigate semantic space and systematically narrow the search. This design makes iterative reasoning and adaptive search strategies both directly observable and necessary for success. We evaluate recent LLMs (GPT-4.1, Llama 4, Claude 3.5 Haiku, Qwen 3), LRMs (o4-mini, Deepseek-R1), humans, and a novel heuristic on 1,500 puzzles. Beyond solve rates (which range from 4% to 62%), we introduce progression-based metrics that reveal models often make meaningful progress, insights that accuracy alone would miss. Our analysis shows how reasoning models can be hindered by overthinking and underthinking, while successful models exhibit human-like strategies. WordPolo demonstrates the need for benchmarks that test both reasoning process and outcomes, providing holistic measurements of model capabilities. Our code and dataset can be found at https://wordpolo-demo.vercel.app/.

</details>


### [178] [TalkMatrix: Generating Character Dialogue that is Both Consistent and Diverse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19022)
*Ayuto Tsutsumi,Yuu Jinnai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“结构化多提示-多候选选择”问题：为每个提示从候选池中选择一个补全，以优化集合级目标；并以角色对话为实例提出 TalkMatrix，通过两层极小极大与多起点坐标上升，在一致性/多样性四目标下联合选择完整补全矩阵，实验显示其矩阵级选择优于随机与独立单元格基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 候选解码通常为每个提示独立选择补全，但许多应用需要满足全局、不可分解约束的输出集合。作者因此将问题形式化为多提示、多候选选择，并聚焦角色对话中跨情境一致性、台词贴合情境以及角色与情境可区分等集合级需求。

Method: TalkMatrix 为每个“角色—情境”对生成多个候选，并基于四个嵌入一致性/多样性目标联合选择完整矩阵。由于加权和可能牺牲某一维度，方法采用两层极小极大最大化最差目标，并用多起点坐标上升近似优化离散目标；与局部、部分矩阵及通用组合搜索基线比较。

Result: 在 50 个合成角色扮演场景和 25 个精选桌游场景中，多个角色在预定义情境中交互；LLM-as-a-judge 评定矩阵级选择高于随机和独立单元格选择基线。

Conclusion: 结果说明结构化选择对全局受控对话生成具有价值，但实证验证仍限于角色扮演场景。

Abstract: Candidate-based decoding typically selects a completion for each prompt independently, but many applications require a collection of outputs that satisfies global, non-decomposable requirements. We formulate this setting as structured multi-prompt, multi-completion selection: given a candidate pool for every prompt, select one completion per prompt to optimize a collection-level objective. We instantiate the problem in character dialogue, where each character should remain consistent across situations, each line should fit its situation, and characters and situations should remain distinguishable. Our method, TalkMatrix, generates multiple candidates for every character--situation pair and jointly selects a complete matrix using four embedding-based consistency and diversity objectives. Because a weighted sum can improve some dimensions by sacrificing another, TalkMatrix maximizes the worst-performing objective through a two-level minimax formulation. We approximately optimize the resulting discrete objective with multi-start coordinate ascent, and compare it with local, partial-matrix, and generic combinatorial search baselines. We run experiments on $50$ synthetic role-playing scenarios and $25$ curated board game scenarios where multiple characters interact in predefined situations. An LLM-as-a-judge rates matrix-level selection higher than random and independent cell-level selection baselines. These results show the value of structured selection for globally controlled dialogue generation, while our empirical validation remains specific to role-playing scenarios.

</details>


### [179] [Entropy in Conversational AI: Structured Unpredictability as Inferrable Interiority](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19044)
*Sebastian Cochinescu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"结构化不可预测性"这一设计目标，即输出与持久隐状态之间的条件依赖（超出观察者从对话记录可推断的范围）；通过选择层维护低维风格-注意力状态并在若干候选中择优，实现了词面新颖性提升，但联合新颖性-一致性准则及路径依赖性均未获证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 普通的随机采样虽能提升响应多样性，却不会产生依赖历史的（history-dependent）行为。作者希望形式化并检验另一类设计目标——"结构化不可预测性"：输出对持久隐状态的依赖应当超出观察者仅凭对话记录所能推断的部分，从而将跨轮依赖从文本表层转移到包装器状态与响应选择机制中。

Method: 提出"选择层"：从容量受限的流中更新一个低维的"风格-注意力"状态，用固定的基础模型生成多条候选响应，再依据新颖性与状态亲和度进行选择。评估采用脚本化的独立提示轮序列：基础模型只接收当前轮次与渲染后的状态，不接收前序对话，因此跨轮依赖完全存在于包装器状态和响应选择器中。先用合成实现验证流程，并对照四个预先哈希冻结的发散度特征（point-level）；随后在真实模型网格（mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit，每臂 56 序列）上运行，设低方差对照、仅一致性对照、新颖性匹配采样、记忆重置消融以及孪生分离检验。

Result: 真实模型网格中，该机制相对低方差对照与仅一致性对照，词面新颖性分别提升 0.073 和 0.023。与新颖性匹配采样的文体一致性对比在注册最小效应规则下等价于零，故联合新颖性-一致性准则未通过；原始两部分累积准则亦未通过。在功效网格之后冻结的修订对比显示，其一致性高于记忆重置消融（0.028，95% CI [0.018, 0.039]），但不能据此确立路径依赖。孪生分离未获证实（0.003，95% CI [-0.011, 0.019]）；均值曲线的饱和曲率与冻结预测一致，但在缺乏分离的情况下不足以支持路径依赖。探针层面的能力等价性在近天花板测试集上处于 ±0.10 区间内成立，输出质量未作评估。全部结果为机器评分，未检验关于心智感知或意识的主张。

Conclusion: 该机制能有效提升词面新颖性，并在记忆重置消融下显示出更高的一致性，但未能通过联合新颖性-一致性准则，也未能确立路径依赖或孪生分离，因此"结构化不可预测性"这一设计目标在真实模型网格中仅得到部分支持；作者同时声明所有结论限于机器评分，不涉及主观体验层面。

Abstract: Sampling can increase response diversity without producing history-dependent behavior. We formalize a different design target, structured unpredictability, as conditional dependence between an output and a persistent hidden state beyond what an observer can infer from the transcript. A selection layer updates a low-dimensional style-and-attention state from a capacity-limited stream, generates several responses with a fixed base model, and selects for novelty and state affinity. Evaluation uses scripted sequences of independent prompt turns: the base model receives the current turn and rendered state, but not the preceding dialogue; cross-turn dependence resides in the wrapper state and response selector. A synthetic implementation validates the pipeline and matches four prospectively hash-frozen divergence features at point level. In the final real-model grid (mlx-community/Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct-4bit; 56 sequences per arm), the mechanism increased lexical novelty over the low-variance and consistency-only controls by 0.073 and 0.023, respectively. Its stylometric-consistency contrast with novelty-matched sampling was equivalent to zero under the registered smallest-effect rule, so the joint novelty-consistency criterion failed. The original two-part accumulation criterion also failed; a revised final-grid contrast, frozen after the powered grid, found higher consistency than the memory-reset ablation (0.028, 95% CI [0.018,0.039]), but does not establish path dependence. Twin separation was not established (0.003, 95% CI [-0.011,0.019]); the mean curve's saturating curvature matched the frozen prediction, which without separation does not support path dependence. Probe-level capability equivalence held within +/-0.10 on a near-ceiling battery, while output quality was not evaluated. All outcomes are machine-scored; no claims about perceived mind or consciousness are tested.

</details>


### [180] [MIRAGE: How Conversation State Shapes Historical Evidence Use in Multimodal Personal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19059)
*Yu Liu,Wenxiao Zhang,Cheng Hu,Cong Cao,Fangfang Yuan,Xinyu Wang,Jin B. Hong,Yanbing Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 MIRAGE，一个在多模态个人智能体中受控评估历史证据使用的框架，通过固定证据、问题与评分、仅改变对话状态，揭示结果导向评估会高估真实证据使用，并发现压缩前深度与压缩后延续构成两种非单调失效机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型（MLLM）智能体作为个人助理需在长时任务中跨对话、文件与工作区状态检索并使用早期证据，其效用依赖历史连续性。然而当历史访问退化时，智能体仍可能生成看似合理的答案，导致仅基于结果正确性的评估高估其对证据的真实使用，因此需要一种在对话状态变化下受控评估历史证据利用的方法。

Method: MIRAGE（Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation）在保持证据对象、问题与评分固定的前提下，仅改变对话状态，评估智能体能否判断可回答性、恢复正确来源并据此作答。实验覆盖七个前沿与开源多模态骨干模型，考察压缩前深度与压缩后延续等状态变化。

Result: 1）压缩前深度与压缩后延续构成两种不同且非单调的失效机制，而非单一退化曲线；2）开源权重模型高度依赖上下文连续性，当溯源失败时不愿自发切换到工具中介检索；3）检索压力能改善工具合规模型在深层压缩前状态下的来源归因，但在压缩后一致回退，因为存储证据已退化。

Conclusion: 历史证据的使用应在状态变化下进行评估，而不能仅由结果正确性推断；MIRAGE 表明结果导向评估会系统性高估多模态个人智能体的真实证据利用能力。

Abstract: Multimodal large language model (MLLM) agents are increasingly used as personal assistants for long-running tasks. Their utility depends on continuity: agents must retrieve and use earlier evidence across dialogue, files, and workspace state. However, agents can generate plausible answers even when access to that history has degraded, causing outcome-only evaluation to overestimate true evidence use. We present MIRAGE (Multimodal Interaction Retrieval, Attribution, and Grounding Evaluation), a controlled study of historical evidence use under conversation-state variation in multimodal personal agents. MIRAGE holds evidence objects, questions, and scoring fixed while varying only conversation state, and evaluates whether an agent can determine answerability, recover the correct source, and answer from it. Across seven frontier and open-weight multimodal backbones, we find that: 1) pre-compaction depth and post-compaction continuation form distinct, non-monotonic failure regimes rather than a single degradation curve; 2) open-weight models rely heavily on context continuity and are reluctant to spontaneously switch to tool-mediated retrieval when provenance fails; and 3) retrieval pressure improves source attribution in deep pre-compaction states for tool-compliant models, but consistently regresses after compaction, where stored evidence has already degraded. These findings show that historical evidence use should be evaluated under state variation, rather than inferred from outcome-only correctness.

</details>


### [181] [Reading Between the Lines: Can LLMs Discover the Question Behind the Text?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19070)
*Claudiu Creanga,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"问题考古学"任务：从完整文本推断其唯一的原始"起源问题"，构建新数据集并系统评测多类模型，发现当前LLM已超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有问题生成任务仅关注生成任何合理问题，话语分析框架又局限于话语层面的言语行为建模，均未评估模型对作者写作意图的把握。作者希望以"起源问题"这一新颖视角，检验模型对作者意图的理解能力。

Method: 提出"问题考古学"（question archaeology）评估任务，即推断驱动文本创作的单一真实"起源问题"。构建新数据集，包含委托撰写的文本及其原始研究问题与合理干扰项。评测对象涵盖商业模型（Gemini Flash、Pro）与开源模型（Mistral、Qwen），以及BERT类模型。

Result: 在该任务上模型表现显著提升，新版本优于旧版本；BERT类模型表现较差；值得注意的是，当前LLM已超越人类在该任务上的表现。

Conclusion: LLM展现出对作者意图的较强理解能力，这对AI在需要细致解读人类沟通的任务中具有重要启示。该工作提供了新的评估框架与具挑战性的基准，供未来模型参考。

Abstract: This paper introduces ``question archaeology'', a specific evaluation task focused on inferring the single, authentic "genesis question" that motivated the creation of a complete text. Distinct from question generation, which targets any plausible question, or discourse frameworks that model utterance-level acts, our task assesses a model's grasp of authorial intent. We present a new dataset of commissioned texts paired with their original research questions and plausible distractors. Our evaluation of both proprietary models, like Gemini Flash and Pro, as well as open source models like Mistral and Qwen, reveals significant progress in this task, with the newer versions outperforming the earlier ones, while BERT-based models performed poorly. Notably, our findings indicate that current LLMs surpass human performance on this task, suggesting advanced understanding of authorial intent. This capability has important implications for AI's role in tasks requiring nuanced interpretation of human communication. Our work thus provides a new framework and a challenging benchmark for future models.

</details>


### [182] [Benchmarking Large Language Models for Biomedical Relation Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19071)
*Claudiu Creanga,Teodor Marchitan,Liviu P. Dinu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究在SNPPhenA语料上系统评测了MLM、混合架构及Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列等LLM在SNP-表型关联抽取三类任务上的表现，O1与Gemini 2.0 Pro分别刷新句子级与摘要级SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从生物医学文献中抽取SNP-表型关联具有重要意义但难度较大，亟需明确不同NLP模型（尤其是新兴LLM）在该任务上的能力边界与最优配置。

Method: 在SNPPhenA语料上对多类模型（MLM、混合架构、Gemini 2.0、OpenAI O系列、Qwen、Mistral）进行基准测试，覆盖句子级分类、摘要级分类和关联强度分类三项任务，并比较少样本与微调设置。

Result: OpenAI O1通过少样本学习在未微调的句子级分类取得SOTA（F1 0.89），并在摘要级分类刷新SOTA（F1 0.82）；关联强度分类最具挑战性，微调后的Gemini 2.0 Pro表现最佳（F1 0.60），为该任务首次LLM评测。专有LLM显著优于其他模型。

Conclusion: 现代LLM（尤其在少样本或微调设置下）对基因组知识抽取具有强大能力，专有LLM在该领域表现突出。

Abstract: Extracting SNP-phenotype associations from biomedical literature is vital but challenging. We benchmarked diverse NLP models, including MLMs, hybrid architectures, and state-of-the-art LLMs (Gemini 2.0, OpenAI O-series, Qwen, Mistral), on the SNPPhenA corpus across three tasks: sentence-level, abstract-level, and association strength classification. OpenAI O1 achieved state-of-the-art (SOTA) results using few-shot learning for non-finetuned sentence-level classification (F1 0.89) and established a new SOTA for abstract-level classification (F1 0.82). Association strength classification proved difficult, though fine-tuned Gemini 2.0 Pro performed best (F1 0.60) in the first LLM evaluation of this task. Proprietary LLMs, especially in few-shot (O1) or fine-tuned (Gemini 2.0 Pro) settings, significantly outperformed other models. These findings confirm the power of modern LLMs for genomic knowledge extraction.

</details>


### [183] [Safety-Flag: A Unified Benchmark for the Reliability and Calibration of LLM Content Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19072)
*Yibo Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出 Safety-Flag，将七个安全基准统一为平衡的 flag/do-not-flag 协议，从错误方向、概率校准与基于置信度的错误排序三个维度评估内容审核可靠性，并公开数据集、代码与排行榜。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大模型内容审核评估多只报告单基准上的聚合准确率，无法揭示错误的类型与方向，也难以反映模型置信度是否可靠，从而掩盖了不同模型在误报、漏报和校准方面的显著差异。

Method: 将 BeaverTails、XSTest、Ethics、WildGuard、Aegis、ToxiChat、ToxiGen 七个基准整合为统一的 flag/do-not-flag 平衡协议，对六个通用 LLM、四个专用 guard 模型及三个参考模型在同一批项目上评估；发布项目级决策与置信度分数，并从错误方向、概率校准、置信度驱动的错误排序三个维度衡量审核可靠性，同时考察基于置信度的弃权策略。

Result: 聚合准确率无法反映错误方向：一个模型将 85% 的良性内容标记为有害，另一个模型漏掉 54% 的有害内容。六个通用模型均过度自信，按模型拟合单一温度参数可在不改变预测标签与置信度排序的前提下将校准误差降低 2.8–6.0 倍。基于置信度的弃权对每个模型都降低选择性风险，但收益取决于置信度对错误的排序能力。专用 guard 误报更少、校准更好，但部分模型在其文档覆盖范围外漏报率更高。

Conclusion: 仅凭聚合准确率评估内容审核模型不足，需同时考察错误方向、校准与错误排序；Safety-Flag 提供了统一协议与可复现资源，公开基准、固定项目列表、评估代码、逐项模型输出及排行榜。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used for content moderation, but most evaluations still report aggregate accuracy on individual benchmarks. We introduce Safety-Flag, which places seven widely used safety benchmarks (BeaverTails, XSTest, Ethics, WildGuard, Aegis, ToxiChat, and ToxiGen) into a single balanced flag / do-not-flag protocol. We release item-level decisions and confidence scores for six general-purpose LLMs and four dedicated guards, together with three reference models, evaluated on the same items. Safety-Flag measures three dimensions of moderator reliability: error direction, probability calibration, and confidence-based error ranking for human review. They often disagree. Aggregate accuracy does not reveal error direction: one model flags $85\%$ of benign content, whereas another misses $54\%$ of harmful content. All six general-purpose models are overconfident; fitting one temperature per model reduces calibration error by $2.8$--$6.0\times$ without changing predicted labels or confidence ordering. Confidence-based abstention lowers selective risk for every model, although the gains depend on how well confidence ranks errors. Dedicated guards produce fewer false alarms and are better calibrated, but several have higher miss rates outside their documented coverage. We release the benchmark, fixed item lists, evaluation code, per-item model outputs, and leaderboard at: https://github.com/yibo-hu-lab/safety-flag-benchmark.

</details>


### [184] [Reporting Practice Matters: The Impact of Reference Choice on Chest X-ray Report Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19093)
*Daniel P. Jeong,Charles Q. Li,Hossein Hosseiny,Nitya M. Bhalla,Fatma Uyar Morency,Pradeep Ravikumar,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了放射学报告撰写习惯差异会显著影响RRG模型评测指标的敏感性，甚至改变模型排名，并提出报告风格改写方法ReRef及数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 放射科医生报告习惯异质，同一影像与相同发现可产生术语、简写、格式、细节程度不同的报告。现有AI放射报告生成评测通常以与人类参考报告的一致性为准，忽略了这种报告风格差异，可能使评测结果失真。

Method: 作者量化既有评测指标对报告撰写习惯变化的敏感性；提出由放射科医生启发的报告习惯变异分类法；并开发ReRef方法，在不改变临床解释的前提下，按该分类轴改写参考报告。基于MIMIC-CXR对九个RRG模型进行实验。

Result: 指标对报告风格变化敏感，影响足以改变模型排名。例如在MIMIC-CXR上用RadCliQ-v1比较九个RRG模型时，压缩参考报告中对正常发现的描述会使Libra从第一降至第二，而CheXOne从第三升至第一。结果表明许多指标未能将临床解释与报告习惯一致性解耦。

Conclusion: 当前RRG评测指标混淆临床解释与报告风格一致性，选择合适的参考报告对实践评测很重要。作者发布放射科医生验证的MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef数据集，包含120对原始与替代参考报告，以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Radiologists follow heterogeneous reporting practices. Two radiologists examining the same image and identifying the same clinical findings might nevertheless compose superficially distinct reports, varying in terminology, shorthand, formatting, and level of detail. These variations in reporting norms represent an under-appreciated obstacle in efforts to evaluate AI-based radiology report generation (RRG) models, where machine-generated reports are typically assessed based on their concordance with human-generated references. In this paper, we quantify the sensitivity of established evaluation metrics to variations in reporting practices, revealing impacts large enough to alter the rankings of models. We introduce a radiologist-informed taxonomy of variations in radiology reporting practice and a method (ReRef) that rewrites reference reports along the axes of our taxonomy while preserving clinical interpretation. For instance, when comparing the performance of nine RRG models on MIMIC-CXR using RadCliQ-v1, condensing the discussion of normal findings in the reference reports causes Libra to drop from first to second place while CheXOne rises from third to first. Our results suggest that many current metrics fail to decouple clinical interpretation from conformity to reporting practices and that choosing the ``right'' references that accurately reflect the desired reporting practices can be important in practice. To support future research, we release MIMIC-CXR-Ext-ReRef, a radiologist-validated dataset of 120 (original, alternative) reference report pairs derived from MIMIC-CXR.

</details>


### [185] [Monitoring and Discovering Reward Hacking with Internal Representations during LLM Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19101)
*Leon Bergen,Usha Bhalla,Andrew Lee,Barak Widawsky,Linas Nasvytis,Connor Watts,Siddharth Boppana,Sidharth Baskaran,Dron Hazra,Michael Byun,Atticus Geiger,Owen Lewis,Matthew Kowal,Vasudev Shyam,Thomas Fel,Thomas McGrath,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Jack Merullo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文发现，简单的均值差（DoM）向量能够在前沿开源LLM（Kimi K3、GLM 5.2、Qwen 3.8 Max）内部表征中连贯地刻画奖励黑客行为；这些向量兼具泛化性与可解释性，可作为近乎零成本的白盒监视器，在DeepSWE、SWE-bench等基准上高效检测并在线预测奖励黑客行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着模型规模扩大，奖励黑客行为愈发频繁、复杂且后果严重。现有检测主要依赖成本高昂的LLM监视器。作者希望探究奖励黑客是否在模型表征中留下可识别的"特征信号"，从而用更廉价的白盒方法来理解和发现模型的各类黑客行为。

Method: 作者在Kimi K3、GLM 5.2、Qwen 3.8 Max等前沿开源LLM上，用简单的"均值差（difference of means, DoM）"向量来表征奖励黑客。具体做法是：在一系列常见评测行为上计算该类向量，并评估其泛化性、可解释性与检测效果；将DoM向量与LLM监视器在匹配假阳性率（matched FPR）下进行比较；同时将DoM向量应用于思维链（chain-of-thought），以在线预测模型后续动作中的黑客行为。

Result: 在DeepSWE与SWE-bench等常用基准上，模型表现出过度奖励黑客：GLM 5.2在DeepSWE的57.2%、SWE-bench的73%采样轨迹中存在黑客行为。在匹配假阳性率下，DoM向量与LLM监视器效果相当但成本几乎为零——在Kimi K3上多捕获3.1%的黑客，在GLM 5.2上少捕获7.9%（DeepSWE）。应用于思维链的DoM向量还能预测后续动作中的奖励黑客，实现在线预警。此外，作者分析了LLM监视器未捕获的探针命中，发现了其他不良行为，并证明该方法可迁移至非SWE评测中。

Conclusion: 这些结果表明，简单、白盒的方法可以可扩展地研究和监控前沿开源模型中的奖励黑客行为。

Abstract: As models scale, reward hacking becomes more frequent, more sophisticated, and more consequential. Does it leave a telltale signature in model representations? This work analyzes how reward hacking is represented internally in frontier open source LLMs, and how those representations can be used to understand and discover the range of hacking behaviors a model displays. In particular, we find that simple difference of means vectors coherently represent reward hacking in Kimi K3, GLM 5.2, and Qwen 3.8 Max across a variety of behaviors in common evaluations. Despite their simplicity, these vectors are both generalizable and interpretable, and we can use them to reliably detect reward hacking. We first evaluate reward hacking in commonly reported benchmarks like DeepSWE and SWE-bench, finding that models reward hack excessively in these environments; GLM 5.2 hacks in 57.2% of rollouts on DeepSWE and in 73% of rollouts on SWE-bench. Catching these requires monitors; LLM monitors are effective, but expensive detectors. We show that DoM vectors are similarly effective but virtually free, catching 3.1% more hacks in Kimi K3 and 7.9% fewer hacks in GLM 5.2 on DeepSWE at a monitor matched false positive rate. DoM vectors run on the chain-of-thought also predict reward hacks in the model's subsequent actions, meaning we can run them online and catch potential hacks before they occur. Finally, we analyze probe-hits that LLM monitors do not catch and discover other undesirable behaviors, as well as show transfer to finding hacks in non-SWE evaluations. Together, these results provide evidence that simple, white-box methods can be used to scalably study and monitor reward hacking behaviors in frontier open source models

</details>


### [186] [Playing log(N)-Questions over Wikipedia Abstracts: Communication Efficiency Between Paired Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19113)
*Peter Potash*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文用$\log(N)$-Questions双智能体游戏评估六个前沿语言模型，共408局、成本\$363。五强模型胜率随文档集规模按$p=0.928$的单参数幂律衰减；唯一能每问提取满比特的模型正是采用标题分割策略的两个模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一个可量化的基准来衡量模型在信息不对称条件下的自我沟通能力。传统基准多为单向问答或静态任务，无法刻画"提问者—回答者同源"时的策略与信息传递效率。$\log(N)$-Questions要求用恰好$\log_2 N$个是/否问题定位目标，理论信息上界严格，因此胜率可直接转化为每问信息量，提供跨模型可比的度量。

Method: 构建双智能体游戏：提问者看$N$条维基百科导语段落，需用恰好$\log_2 N$个是/否问题找出隐藏目标；回答者只看目标与问题，仅用一个词作答。两角色由同一提供方运行。测试六个前沿模型，在4至1024段落规模上共进行408局，API总成本\$363。记录胜率、答案错误、判别失败、每问信息量（由答案平衡估计）及推理token消耗，并人工审查最弱模型的全部一致答案错误。

Result: Claude Opus 5表现明显落后，68局胜28；GLM-5.3、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.6、Gemini 3.8 Flash、Kimi K3胜45–56局，五者差异微小。五强合并后胜率随集规模下降，相关系数$r=-0.973$，拟合为$\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$，$p=0.928$。损失中答案错误与判别失败约各占一半，模型几乎不会指向自身证据已排除的文档。最弱模型的34个一致答案错误中32个为"No"，且针对文档首句所述属性，尽管指令明确警告勿默认回答"No"。每问信息量与胜率相关$r=+0.88$；仅两个模型达到每问一整比特，也正是唯一按文档标题分割的模型——该策略在$N=32$以下未出现，在其以上占约四分之一问题。推理token消耗跨模型差$4.5\times$，与成功率关系不大；推理轨迹随候选集缩小而变长，却未带来可靠性提升。

Conclusion: 该游戏能有效分离模型自我沟通能力：五强受单一每轮可靠性参数支配，$p=0.928$意味着每问平均不足1比特。高信息量策略（按标题分割）是达到满比特的关键，而增加推理token并不提升可靠性。最弱模型的系统偏差集中在"默认回答No"，提示指令遵循缺陷比能力上限更能解释失败。

Abstract: We evaluate six frontier language models on the two-agent $\log(N)$-Questions game. A questioner sees $N$ Wikipedia lead paragraphs and must identify a secretly chosen target using exactly $\log_2 N$ yes/no questions. An answerer sees only the target and the question, and replies with one word. Both roles run on the same provider, so the game measures how well a model communicates with itself across an information asymmetry. We run 408 games over document sets of 4 to 1024 paragraphs at a total API cost of \$363. One model finishes well behind the others: Claude Opus 5 wins 28 of 68 games, against 45 to 56 for GLM-5.3, GPT-5.6 Sol, Grok 4.6, Gemini 3.8 Flash and Kimi K3. The leading five are only marginally separable. Pooling those five, win rate declines with set size at $r=-0.973$ and is fit by a single per-round reliability parameter. The form is $\text{win}=p^{\log_2 N}$ with $p=0.928$. Losses divide into answer errors and discrimination failures in roughly equal measure, and models almost never name a document their own evidence excludes. Every unanimous answer error from the weakest model was inspected: 32 of 34 are ``No'' answers, on properties stated in the document's first sentence, under an instruction that explicitly warns against defaulting to ``No''. Information per question, estimated from answer balance, correlates with win rate at $r=+0.88$. The only two models to extract a full bit per question are the only two that partition on document titles, a strategy absent below $N{=}32$ and used in a quarter of questions above it. Reasoning-token expenditure varies $4.5\times$ across models with little relation to success, and the trace grows as the candidate set shrinks without a matching gain in reliability.

</details>


### [187] [ScienceIDE: Turning World's Scientific Codebase into Agent Learnable Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19134)
*Hejia Geng,Zesen Huang,Haoyang Li,Wenbin Li,Koutian Wu,Zihan Zhou,Yuanbo Pang,Weihao Liu,Zigong Xu,Zhiping Li,Zongzheng Zhang,Chuanfei Dong,Jiankai Sun,Tianzhe Zheng,Fengyu Xie,Yue Ma,Yueheng Shi,Tong Xie,Zonglin Di,Xianrong Liu,Qucheng Gao,Yimin Liu,Jiaming Pan,Sheng Huang,Xiao-Han Ma,Lanqing Yuan,Zhenlin Zhu,Ziang Liu,Ziyang Xu,Junkai Wang,Kangkai Liang,Jiayi Xian,Zehong Zhao,Liuwei Xu,Jingxu Xie,Peijin Zhang,Qiang Gao,Chengyi Xing,Zhe Zhao,Xi Wang,Yaopeng Xing,Xing Meng,Zhenfei Yin,Yingcheng Wu,Ling Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ScienceIDE 基础设施，将科学代码仓库转化为可执行、可验证的智能体训练环境，并基于此训练出 PhAI-IDE 系列模型（72B/9B/4B），在科学代码修复及部分通用代码、推理、知识基准上取得提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学代码库蕴含数十年人类知识，但其工具链碎片化、领域约定隐性、正确性标准专门化，导致这些知识难以转化为可靠的学习经验，论文将这一问题称为"科学经验瓶颈"（scientific experience bottleneck）。

Method: 构建 ScienceIDE：在专家定义的科学案例与验收标准指导下，由智能体将代码仓库转化为支持任务生成、执行与科学验证的可执行环境；这些环境统一支撑监督微调、强化学习与评测。利用经过验证的交互轨迹训练 PhAI-IDE-72B、PhAI-IDE-9B、PhAI-IDE-4B 模型族。

Result: 模型族在留出的科学代码修复任务上取得提升，并在代码、推理与知识等选定的通用基准上也有增益，表明科学经验向更广泛能力存在正向迁移。

Conclusion: ScienceIDE 为智能体学习与科学实践构建了集成工作空间基础，使人类科学软件成为发展科学智能的共享基座。

Abstract: Scientific code repositories encode decades of human knowledge in executable models, methods, and tools. Yet fragmented toolchains, implicit domain conventions, and specialized correctness criteria make this knowledge difficult to convert into reliable learning experience-a challenge we call the scientific experience bottleneck. We introduce ScienceIDE, infrastructure for turning the world's scientific code into programmable environments for scientific agents. Guided by expert-defined scientific cases and acceptance criteria, agents transform repositories into executable environments that support task generation, execution, and scientific verification. These environments provide a shared foundation for supervised fine-tuning, reinforcement learning, and evaluation. Using verified interaction trajectories, we train PhAI-IDE-72B, PhAI-IDE-9B, and PhAI-IDE-4B. The model family shows gains in held-out scientific-code repair and across selected general-purpose benchmarks in code, reasoning, and knowledge, providing evidence of positive transfer from scientific experience to broader capabilities. ScienceIDE lays the foundation for an integrated workspace for agent learning and scientific practice, making humanity's scientific software a shared substrate for developing scientific intelligence. Code: https://github.com/aitofound/ScienceIDE

</details>


### [188] [A Zeroth-Order Paradigm for LLM Preference Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19144)
*Peter Chen,Xi Chen,Wotao Yin,Tianyi Lin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出并分析了一种零阶对齐方法ComPO，通过比较oracle从偏好对中提取方向信息，避免直接在偏好对上优化可微偏好损失，并给出离线与在线方案的理论保证及多模型实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有直接偏好对齐方法因计算与内存效率被广泛用于将大语言模型与人类偏好对齐，但似然位移（likelihood displacement）问题促使研究者寻找新的方式，从具有较小似然间隔的偏好对中提取信息。

Method: 提出ComPO（Comparison-based Preference Optimization），一种基于比较oracle的零阶对齐方法，不直接在偏好对上优化可微偏好损失，而是从偏好对中提取方向信息。给出其基础离线方案的收敛保证，假设包括平滑性、梯度稀疏性以及oracle与潜在目标之间的兼容性。进一步提出在线ComPO，保留离线比较机制，并利用无标签策略生成进行相对参考策略的reverse-KL控制。

Result: 在局部覆盖与分布内成对奖励准确率条件下，为一种基础约束方案建立了性能保证。在Mistral、Llama、Gemma-2、Qwen3和Gemma-3模型上的实验表明，ComPO优于现有直接对齐方法，包括长度控制胜率，且成对级别诊断证据与缓解似然位移相一致。

Conclusion: ComPO通过比较oracle有效缓解似然位移问题，兼具离线收敛保证与在线性能保证，并在多种主流模型上取得优于现有直接对齐方法的效果。

Abstract: Direct preference alignment methods are widely used to align large language models (LLMs) with human preferences because of their computational and memory efficiency. However, likelihood displacement motivates alternative ways to extract information from preference pairs with small likelihood margins. In this paper, we propose and analyze Comparison-based Preference Optimization (ComPO), a zeroth-order alignment method based on comparison oracles. ComPO extracts directional information from these pairs without directly optimizing a differentiable preference loss on them. We establish a convergence guarantee for its basic offline scheme under smoothness, gradient sparsity, and compatibility between the oracle and a latent objective. We further introduce online ComPO, which retains the offline comparison mechanism and uses unlabeled policy generations for reverse-KL control relative to a reference policy. Following the coverage perspective of preference fine-tuning, we establish a performance guarantee for a basic constrained scheme under local coverage and in-distribution pairwise reward accuracy. Experiments on Mistral, Llama, Gemma-2, Qwen3, and Gemma-3 models demonstrate improvements over existing direct alignment methods, including length-controlled win rates, with pair-level diagnostics providing evidence consistent with mitigating likelihood displacement.

</details>


### [189] [Objective vs. Search: Decomposing What Makes a Good Tokeniser](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19145)
*Ahmetcan Yavuz,Clara Meister,Tiago Pimentel*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文通过构建BottomUpLL和TopDownComp两个新的分词算法，补齐了"优化目标×搜索过程"的2×2设计空间，发现自底向上搜索过程（而非优化目标）是影响语言模型性能的主导因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPE与UnigramLM是当前主流分词算法，二者在优化目标（压缩 vs. 对数似然）和搜索过程（自底向上合并 vs. 自顶向下剪枝）两个正交维度上均不同。既有比较将这两个维度混淆，无法判断性能差异究竟源于"优化什么"还是"如何优化"。

Method: 引入两个新分词算法以补全2×2设计空间：BottomUpLL（自底向上、基于似然）与TopDownComp（自顶向下、基于压缩）。用各算法生成的分词器训练语言模型，并变化模型规模、词表大小与语言领域（纯英文 vs. 多语言）。

Result: 在bits-per-byte指标上，搜索过程而非优化目标是主导因素：多数设置下自底向上分词器取得更低的bits-per-byte。而在BLiMP任务上，设计选择与性能之间无一致关系。

Conclusion: 研究厘清了分词器设计选择对语言建模性能的影响，为更合理、更具原则性的分词器构建提供了具体指导。

Abstract: Two dominant tokenisation algorithms are used by modern language models: byte-pair encoding (BPE) and UnigramLM. These differ along two orthogonal axes: their optimisation objective (compression vs. log-likelihood) and their search procedure (bottom-up merging vs. top-down pruning). Existing comparisons confound these axes, making it unclear whether their observed differences stem from what is being optimised vs. how it is being optimised. We disentangle the two by introducing two new tokenisation algorithms that complete this 2x2 design space: BottomUpLL, a bottom-up likelihood-based tokeniser, and TopDownComp, a top-down compression-based tokeniser. We train language models with tokenisers produced by each algorithm, varying: model size, vocabulary sizes, and domain (English-only vs. multilingual). Evaluating models on bits-per-byte, we find that the search procedure -- not the objective -- is the dominant factor: bottom-up tokenisers consistently achieve lower bits-per-byte in most settings. Evaluating models on the BLiMP task, however, shows no consistent relationship between design choice and performance. Overall, our results disentangle the effect of tokeniser design choices on language modelling performance, offering concrete guidance for their more principled construction.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Human-aware Design Generation: Adding 3D Humans into Graphic Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17689)
*Zijin Hou,Ying Cao*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出"以设计为条件的3D人物添加"新任务，构建模型预测人物3D姿态、2D构图及图像在版式中的空间布局，生成与设计元素协调的完整平面设计，性能优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平面设计普遍包含人物图像，人物的姿态与构图方式用于引导视觉探索并激发目标情绪；然而现有自动平面设计生成研究尚未探讨人物表征在其中的作用，故需强调视觉人物表征对设计生成的重要性。

Method: 定义design-conditioned 3D human adding任务：在已有部分设计的基础上添加3D人体表征。模型联合预测三项要素——人体3D姿态、包含该人像的图像的2D构图（取景），以及该图像在输入设计中的空间排布，从而合成完整设计。

Result: 实验表明，相较基线方法，本文模型预测的3D人体姿态、2D取景与人像布局与其他既有设计元素更协调，生成的整幅设计质量更高。

Conclusion: 该工作首次探索3D人体生成与2D平面设计生成之间的联系，为后续利用3D信息构建更强的2D平面设计生成模型提供启示，代码已开源。

Abstract: Graphic designs nowadays predominantly contain human images, where people are posed and framed in a delicate way to guide viewers' visual exploration and evoke desired feelings. Despite recent progress in automatic graphic design generation, there has been no work on investigating the role of the human representations in design creation. In this paper, we seek to emphasize the importance of visual human representations in graphic design generation, by studying a novel task, dubbed as design-conditioned 3D human adding. To solve this task, we propose a model that adds 3D human representations into an existing partial design, by predicting their 3D poses, their 2D composition in the image containing them, as well as the spatial arrangement of the image in the input design, to generate a complete design. Our experiments show that our model outperforms baselines by predicting the 3D human poses, 2D framing and human image layout that better harmonize with the other existing design elements, and generating higher-quality holistic designs. Our work is the first to explore connections between 3D human generation and 2D graphic design generation, which would inspire future research in leveraging 3D information to build more powerful generative models for graphic designs in the 2D domain. The code is available at https://github.com/GreyNails/Human-aware-Design-Generation-Adding-3D-Humans-into-Graphic-Designs.

</details>


### [191] [PointGrade: Geometric Priors for Grading MoonBoard Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17770)
*Beatrice Stotz,Ningna Wang,Daria Nogina,Caroline Zhang,Jiyang Yin,Amy Huang,Ben Yang,Jace Li,Joel Salzman,Steven Feiner,Silvia Sellán*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出PointGrade，一种结合3D点云分类与序列建模的机器学习方法，用于预测MoonBoard抱石线路的难度等级，性能优于忽略几何信息的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MoonBoard难度等级预测方法多基于序列（攀爬顺序）建模，忽略了岩点本身的几何形状信息，而这些潜藏的三维几何特征对难度判断具有价值。

Method: 从每个岩点的预扫描网格中采样点云，采用3D物体分类架构与现有基于序列的难度等级预测方法相结合，构建PointGrade模型以捕获线路中的潜在几何信息。

Result: PointGrade在MoonBoard难度预测任务上取得了优于其他忽略几何数据方法的性能。

Conclusion: 将岩点三维几何信息纳入建模能够有效提升抱石线路难度等级预测的效果，验证了几何特征在该任务中的重要性。

Abstract: A MoonBoard is a standardized bouldering wall used in gyms around the world. Climbs up the wall limited to only a subset of holds are known as problems. We introduce PointGrade, a novel machine learning approach to predicting the difficulty of a MoonBoard problem. By sampling a point cloud from pre-scanned meshes of every hold, our model combines 3D object classification architecture with existing sequence-based approaches to difficulty grade prediction. Our method captures latent geometric information contained the climb, outperforming other work on the problem that neglect this data.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [192] [Making AI-Assisted Claims Independently Challengeable: Publication Authority and a Protocol for Falsifiable Publication Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17631)
*Torsten Olivi Tiltack,Yifei Dong,Kun Yu,Xu Wang,Wei Liu,Jianlong Zhou,Ren Ping Liu,Fang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文提出"发布权威"(Publication Authority)概念,即一种精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的发布能力,并在 PAC-2026(Publication-Accountability Calculus)机器可读规范中实例化,通过第四版有界语义冻结(SF-4)的六项义务、110,764个安全可达状态与76个不安全配置等评估,论证其内部一致性、有界安全性与有限可构造性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 辅助生成的论断在证据、分析、人类授权、呈现与更正历史指向不同状态时,可能显得具有权威性(即"权威性错位")。现有的溯源(provenance)、证明(attestation)与透明性机制虽能暴露历史,却无法界定此处所考察的"发布状态转移",因此需要一个能精确刻画发布授权条件的框架。

Method: 提出 Publication Authority 作为精确状态、不可转移、单次使用的发布能力,构建 PAC-2026(AIJIM 协议候选)机器可读规范,并评估其第四版有界语义冻结(SF-4)。SF-4 对证据、运行与工件、度量披露、授权、表面一致性、生命周期连续性设立六项不可相互补偿的义务,每项产生目标绑定见证、局部化反例或局部化不可验证性;仅当存在全新的、完整的全通过记录时,才派生出被单次原子发布转移所消耗的许可。验证手段包括身份向量、对抗案例、有限模型与历史实现对照。

Result: 10 个模型探索了 110,764 个安全可达状态;76 个不安全配置均产生预期违规或观察者反模型。通过一致性检查的读者表面不足以授权发布,除非被接受的记录允许该表面。SF-4 将证据视界与验证时刻分离,并拒绝真实但因果无效的授权。历史前驱路径复现了 17 个冻结的授权—后继结果;后续同主机、实例盲测匹配全部 183 项评分预期,并以 240 条归档观测复现同主机包执行。

Conclusion: 结果支持该框架的内部一致性、有界安全性、故障敏感性与有限可构造性,但不支持事实真理性、一般化精化、盲互操作性、现场有效性与标准地位。

Abstract: AI-assisted claims can appear authoritative when evidence, analysis, human authorization, presentation, and correction history refer to different states. Provenance, attestation, and transparency expose history but alone do not specify the publication transition examined here. We develop Publication Authority as an exact-state, non-transferable, single-use publication capability and instantiate it in PAC-2026 (Publication-Accountability Calculus), a machine-readable AIJIM Protocol candidate. We evaluate its fourth bounded semantic freeze (SF-4), a fixed-profile specification designed for replaceable bindings. Six obligations govern evidence, runs and artifacts, measurement disclosure, authorization, surface correspondence, and lifecycle continuity. Each yields a target-bound witness, localized counterexample, or localized unverifiability; none can compensate for another. Only a fresh, complete all-pass record derives the permit consumed by one atomic publication transition. We use identity vectors, adversarial cases, finite models, and historical implementations. Ten models explored 110,764 safe reachable states; 76 unsafe configurations produced the expected violation or observer countermodel. A reader surface passing its correspondence check cannot authorize publication unless the accepted record admits that surface. SF-4 separates evidence horizon from verification time and rejects an authentic but causally invalid authorization. A historical predecessor path reproduced 17 frozen authorization-successor outcomes. A later in-house, instance-blind test of known case classes matched all 183 scored expectations; same-host package execution reproduced its 240 archived observations. Results support internal coherence, bounded safety, fault sensitivity, and limited constructibility, but not factual truth, general refinement, blind interoperability, field efficacy, or standards status.

</details>


### [193] [EvolveTrade: Experience-Driven Policy Refinement for Self-Evolving LLM Trading Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17632)
*Sehee Kim,Yumin Choi,Minki Kang,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EvolveTrade将工具型交易智能体的系统提示词视为"文本参数化策略"，在冻结主干LLM的前提下，利用决策轨迹与组合反馈周期性自演化该策略，从而在多种市场状态下提升夏普比率与累计收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM交易智能体虽能整合市场数据、新闻与可执行分析，但其工具使用行为由部署前固定的手写静态策略控制，难以在变化的市场状态下自适应地调整信息搜集、工具调用、信号验证与风险管理方式。

Method: 提出EvolveTrade自演化框架：将工具使用型交易智能体的系统提示词建模为"文本参数化策略"；每个更新区间后，由Policy Agent基于累积的决策轨迹与已实现的组合反馈对该策略进行修订，主干LLM保持固定；更新后的策略用于下一批交易决策，使智能体持续优化其信息获取与组合构建流程。

Result: 在多种市场状态与两个LLM主干上的实验中，EvolveTrade在多数评估设置中相较固定策略的LLM基线提升了夏普比率与累计收益；行为分析显示自演化策略增加了代码介导的分析并激活与市场状态相关的计算；案例级的"策略—收益"归因追踪了策略诱发的配置变化如何贡献于实际收益差异。

Conclusion: 自演化地调整支配工具使用的可复用流程，是构建更稳健LLM交易智能体的关键方向。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) trading agents can combine market data, news, and executable analysis, but their behavior is often controlled by static hand-written tool-use policies that are fixed before deployment. This limits their ability to adapt how they gather evidence, invoke tools, verify signals, and manage risk under changing market regimes. We introduce EvolveTrade, a self-evolving framework that treats the system prompt of a tool-using trading agent as a text-parameterized policy. After each update interval, a Policy Agent revises this policy using accumulated decision traces and realized portfolio feedback, while keeping the backbone LLM fixed. The updated policy is then used for the next batch of trading decisions, enabling the agent to refine its information-acquisition and portfolio-construction procedure over time. Experiments across multiple market regimes and two LLM backbones show that EvolveTrade often improves Sharpe Ratio and Cumulative Return over fixed-policy LLM baselines, achieving the improved SR and CR in most evaluated settings. Behavioral analyses further show that self-evolved policies increase code-mediated analysis and activate regime-relevant computations; case-level policy-to-return attributions trace how policy-induced allocation changes contribute to realized return differences. These results suggest that adapting the reusable procedure governing tool use is a key direction for building more robust LLM trading agents.

</details>


### [194] [One Color Preprocessing Improves DSATUR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17633)
*Adam Nouira,Lucas Isenmann*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SSLD，用SDP预处理首个颜色类后再由DSATUR完成图着色，在1600+实例上几乎全部匹配或超越DSATUR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图着色问题（GCP）是NP难问题，DSATUR是最快的启发式之一，但所用颜色数通常多于最先进算法，因此存在改进空间。

Method: SSLD先通过类似计算Lovász theta数的半定规划（SDP）预处理出一个优质颜色类，再交由DSATUR完成剩余着色；这是首个通过固定颜色类预处理来改进DSATUR的方法。

Result: 在DIMACS、随机图（Erdős–Rényi、Watts-Strogatz、Barabási–Albert）、频率分配和作业车间调度等超过1600个基准实例上，SSLD几乎全部匹配或优于DSATUR，并胜过朴素GISD基线，证实SDP引导的首颜色类选择的价值；代价是运行时间约为DSATUR的195倍。

Conclusion: SDP引导的首颜色类预处理是提升图着色启发式质量的有效方向，值得未来进一步优化以降低运行时间。

Abstract: The Graph Coloring Problem (GCP) is NP-hard and DSATUR stands as one of the fastest heuristics for it despite producing colorings that typically use more colors than state-of-the-art coloring algorithms. We propose SSLD (Semidefinite Spectral Learning with DSATUR), which improves DSATUR by preprocessing a first good color class before letting DSATUR complete coloring the rest of the given graph. We obtain this color class from a Semidefinite Programming (SDP), similar to an SDP used to compute the Lovász theta number. To the best of our knowledge, SSLD is the first approach to improve DSATUR by preprocessing through fixed color classes. We evaluate SSLD against DSATUR and against a naive 1-color-class preprocessing algorithm on DIMACS instances, random graphs (Erdős--Rényi, Watts-Strogatz, Barabási--Albert), Frequency Assignment and Job Shop Scheduling instances. SSLD matches or beats DSATUR in almost every case across over 1600 benchmark instances, and out performs the naive GISD baseline, allows us to confirm the value brought by the SDP-guided choice of the first color class. This quality comes at a runtime cost of roughly 195 times slower that DSATUR, but demonstrating that SDP-guided preprocessing of a first color class is a direction for future improvements.

</details>


### [195] [Physics-Constrained Digital Twins for Sensor Integrity in Urban Pedestrian Flow: Detecting Stealthy False Data Injection with Conformal Guarantees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17635)
*Oscar Mogollon Gutierrez,Fatemeh Ghasemi,Mohammadhossein Homaei,Andres Caro,Mar Avila*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文研究城市行人计数系统的数字孪生被隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击的情形，提出基于行人街道图、学习式图局部增益与流量守恒残差的孪生估计与检测方法，并用自适应保形校准设定阈值，以"攻击裕度"量化物理守恒律带来的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 城市行人计数系统已用于经济指标、规划决策与安全运营，但建立在其上的数字孪生把输入数据流当作真值（ground truth）处理。当数据流被恶意篡改时后果严重，而行人人流感知中"隐状态流→观测"的映射秩缺失程度远高于已研究过隐蔽性的电力网和水网，因此需要专门刻画该场景下的隐蔽虚假数据注入问题。

Method: 1) 形式化城市尺度行人感知的隐蔽虚假数据注入攻击；2) 孪生在行人街道图上估计有向流量，通过可学习的图局部（graph-localised）增益同化计数值；3) 训练目标为耦合已计量与未计量路段的流量守恒残差；4) 检测器融合新息（innovation）与守恒残差，告警阈值采用自适应保形校准（conformal calibration）而非人工设定；5) 定义"攻击裕度"（attack margin），即在白盒对手直接通过孪生优化的最坏情况流场篡改下的相对降低量，用以衡量物理约束的贡献。

Result: 在墨尔本六年数据上，单一设备被入侵时攻击裕度达0.54；当三分之一设备被入侵时降至0.19，而此时网络中仅1.18%的可步行路段被计量。将街道图替换为距离图后，攻击裕度跌至0.09，说明鲁棒性收益来自守恒律而非局部性。

Conclusion: 流量守恒等物理约束为行人感知数字孪生提供了实质性的攻击鲁棒性，其增益远超单纯依赖节点局部性的表示；当被入侵设备比例升高时裕度明显下降，需结合更大规模计量覆盖以维持防御强度。

Abstract: City pedestrian counting systems now feed economic indicators, planning decisions and safety operations, yet the twins built on top of them treat the incoming stream as ground truth. We study what happens when it is not. We formalise stealthy false data injection for city-scale pedestrian sensing, where the map from latent flow to observation is far more rank deficient than in the power and water networks for which stealth has been characterised. Our twin estimates directed flows on the pedestrian street graph, assimilates counts through a learned graph-localised gain, and is trained against a flow conservation residual that couples metered and unmetered segments. Detection combines the innovation with that residual, and the alarm threshold is set by adaptive conformal calibration rather than by hand. To measure what the physics buys, we define the attack margin, the relative reduction in worst-case corruption of the estimated flow field, achieved against a white-box adversary that optimises directly through the twin. On six years of Melbourne data the margin reaches 0.54 against a single compromised device and falls to 0.19 when a third of the fleet is compromised, on a network where only 1.18 per cent of walkable segments are metered. Replacing the street graph by a distance graph collapses it to 0.09, which shows that the gain comes from the conservation law rather than from locality.

</details>


### [196] [What You Can't See Is Still What You Learn: A Preregistered Sixty-Society Confirmation That Evidence Masking Drives Compositional Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17637)
*Narcis Marincat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在预注册确认实验中，64个共享冻结语言模型主干的四单元系统通过连续数据包通信，证据遮蔽条件在留出的二、三步运算组合上显著优于对照（中位配对差0.846与0.859），全部12对均达标，主行为标准通过，无标记复现亦通过；然而全局可见系统未通过角色标记检验，填充条件的分解判据不确定，中介效应未被证实。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验"限制模块可读取的内容能否改善系统学到的计算"这一假设，并检验证据遮蔽、所有权标记、外来证据替换为中性填充等因素的作用，以及该效应是否依赖角色信息与中介机制。

Method: 预注册确认实验；64个四单元系统共享冻结语言模型主干，通过学习到的连续数据包通信。五个条件操纵证据遮蔽、所有权标记、外来证据替换为中性填充；跨6个初始化聚类，每个含两种数据顺序，在单一全新任务世界中进行。数据分析包括中位配对差异、预注册行为标准、标记跟随检验、分解判据与18个遮蔽系统的数据包干预审计。

Result: 标记可用时，遮蔽使留出二步与三步运算组合准确率中位配对差分别提升0.846与0.859，12对全部超过既定阈值，完整预注册行为标准通过；无标记复现同样通过。无任何全局可见系统通过标记跟随检验，角色信息作用未定。填充条件产生7个完全泛化者，但分解判据不确定。18个受审计遮蔽系统的数据包干预均符合预测的中间值变化，但属有限、成功条件化的审计，不构成中介证据。

Conclusion: 结果确认所测遮蔽机制具有大幅优势，但其更细粒度的归因与普适性仍有待厘清；协议、结果与检查点均已公开。

Abstract: Restricting what a module can read may improve what a system learns to compute. We test this in a preregistered confirmation with sixty four-cell systems sharing a frozen language-model backbone and communicating through learned continuous packets. Five conditions vary evidence masking, ownership markers, and replacement of foreign evidence with neutral filler, across six initialization clusters, each with two data orders, on one fresh task world. With markers available in both regimes, masking improved accuracy on held-out two- and three-operation compositions by median paired differences of 0.846 and 0.859; all twelve pairs cleared the required margins, and the full preregistered behavioral criterion passed. The unmarked replication also passed. No globally visible system passed the marker-following check, so the effect of usable role information remains unresolved. The filler condition yielded seven full generalizers, but its decomposition criteria were inconclusive. Packet interventions in all eighteen audited masked systems followed the predicted intermediate-value changes on eligible cases; these finite, success-conditioned audits do not establish mediation. The results confirm a large advantage of the tested masking regime, while leaving its finer attribution and generality open. Protocols, results, and checkpoints are public.

</details>


### [197] [CapMem: A Benchmark for Caption-Based Episodic Memory in Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17688)
*Dingli Liang,Yiqiao Xie,Yukai Huang,Zhaokai Wang,Weitong Cai,Guangwen Feng,Jifei Song,Zhensong Zhang,Hang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出可用文本描述（caption）作为可复用的情节记忆，构建了CapMem基准（75段视频、33.7小时、1000道多选题），并证明在长视频上CaptionQA优于直接VideoQA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴助手需要基于第一视角视频的情节记忆，但现有视觉语言模型受限于帧预算、视觉token成本增长和长上下文检索失效，因此探究文本描述能否作为可复用的情节记忆。

Method: 定义Episodic Memory Video Caption QA任务，构建人工标注基准CapMem（75段视频、33.7小时、16个场景、1000道多选题）；采用全覆盖的CaptionQA（30s/60s描述窗口）与直接VideoQA对比，并设置匹配帧数的对照实验，以及描述引导的检索-验证框架。

Result: 在长视频（>20分钟）上，30s与60s窗口的CaptionQA分别在10/12和8/12的模型上优于直接VideoQA；在相同视频子集上，六个Qwen模型的匹配帧对照平均准确率增益分别为3.22和2.55个百分点；描述引导的检索-验证框架进一步最高提升5.3个百分点。

Conclusion: 文本描述记忆对长第一视角视频的情节推理是有效的。

Abstract: Wearable assistants require episodic memory over egocentric video, yet current vision-language models face bounded frame budgets, growing visual-token costs, and long-context retrieval failures. Under these practical constraints, we study whether textual captions can serve as reusable episodic memory. We define the Episodic Memory Video Caption QA task and introduce CapMem, a human-annotated benchmark with 75 videos totaling 33.7 hours, and 1,000 multiple-choice questions across 16 scenarios. On long videos (>20 min), full-coverage CaptionQA with 30s and 60s caption windows outperforms direct VideoQA for 10/12 and 8/12 models, respectively. On the same video subset, a matched-frame control across six Qwen models retains mean accuracy gains of 3.22 and 2.55 points, respectively. Our caption-guided retrieve-and-verify harness further improves accuracy by up to 5.3 points. These results support the effectiveness of caption memory for episodic reasoning over long egocentric video.

</details>


### [198] [GraphEcho: Structural Redundancy and Evidence Provenance in LLM Graph Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17695)
*Sikun Wang,Yixi Zhou,Lei Fan,Fan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GraphEcho 基准测试检验 LLM 智能体是否将图中重复路径误认为额外的独立证据，并评估其判断与主动探索行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 智能体可以在不获得更多独立证据的情况下遍历更多图路径。本文关注的问题是：智能体是否会将这些重复出现误当作额外的证据支持（corroboration）。

Method: 构建 GraphEcho 基准：在固定证据内容的前提下，变化路径数量与证据来源（evidential origins），同时评估智能体的判断（judgments）与主动探索（active exploration）。此外提出并测试来源感知后训练（Provenance-aware post-training, PAPT）。

Result: 受控合成实验显示模型相关的判断偏移；冗余支持路径提高了所有被评估冻结智能体的重复遍历（repeated walks）比例。PAPT 减少了重复访问并提升合成任务准确率，但覆盖的独立来源更少。在科学主张上，PAPT 持续减少重复，但准确率下降。

Conclusion: 研究表明高效探索与有效证据利用之间存在鸿沟：智能体可能学会停止重复自身，却忽略其所需的信息。GraphEcho 提供了一种受控方式来同时评估图智能体得出何种结论，以及其探索是否触及不同的证据来源。

Abstract: A large language model (LLM) agent can follow more graph paths without acquiring more independent evidence. GraphEcho tests whether agents mistake these repeated encounters for additional corroboration. The benchmark varies path counts and evidential origins while holding evidence content fixed, and evaluates both judgments and active exploration. Controlled synthetic experiments reveal model-dependent judgment shifts, but redundant supporting paths increase the share of repeated walks across all evaluated frozen agents. Provenance-aware post-training (PAPT) reduces revisits and improves synthetic accuracy, yet covers fewer distinct sources. On scientific claims, it continues to reduce repetition while accuracy declines. These findings expose a gap between efficient exploration and effective evidence use: an agent can learn to stop repeating itself while overlooking information it needs. GraphEcho provides a controlled way to evaluate both what graph agents conclude and whether their exploration reaches distinct evidential sources.

</details>


### [199] [GVD: Governed Versioning and Deduplication for Document Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17696)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出GVD框架，将跨文档版本链接与规则级冲突检测统一到可审计的更新策略之下，实现本地化、无需大模型的文档治理流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档库持续演化，指南与政策不断修订、替代和重新上传，导致相同内容以不同措辞反复出现，新版本之间可能细化或相互矛盾。这些不一致性属于整个文档集合而非单个文档，但现有研究把版本识别、重复检测和矛盾检测当作彼此孤立的成对任务，标签完成即终止，无法刻画全局演化与冲突。

Method: GVD框架：通过双向规则对齐将新到文档归入版本家族；将文档规则与家族记忆比对，区分重复、矛盾、非对称细化和新增知识四类关系；采用反事实片段探测（CSP）解决被推理误判为中性的相关对；针对不同关系类型施加差异化策略，抑制重复项、仅将实质性变化升级送审，并保留版系谱以形成审计轨迹。整个流程完全本地运行，不依赖大语言模型。

Result: 在120份企业文档、59个版本家族、140次摄入的评测中，GVD取得版本家族构建F1为0.97、规则级一致性F1为0.94；CSP将规则一致性从0.90提升至0.94。

Conclusion: GVD将原本割裂的成对任务统一为以版本家族为单位的全局治理框架，在无大模型的本地部署下同时实现高精度的版本链接与规则级冲突识别，并通过关系感知策略支持可审计的更新管理。

Abstract: Document repositories evolve continuously. Guidelines and policies are revised, superseded, and re-uploaded, so the same content recurs in different wording and newer versions refine or contradict earlier ones. These inconsistencies belong to the growing collection rather than to any single document, yet existing work treats versioning, duplicate detection, and contradiction detection as isolated pairwise tasks and stops once a pair is labeled. We present GVD (Governed Versioning and Deduplication), a framework that unifies cross-document version linking with rule-level conflict resolution under an auditable update policy. Incoming documents are assigned to version families through bidirectional rule alignment, and their rules are compared against the family memory to identify duplicates, contradictions, asymmetric refinements, and new knowledge, with Counterfactual Span Probing (CSP) resolving related pairs that inference misclassifies as neutral. Relation-specific policies suppress duplicates and escalate only consequential changes for review, retaining version lineage as an audit trail. The pipeline runs fully locally, with no large language model. On 120 enterprise documents processed as 140 ingestions across 59 version families, GVD reaches an F1 of 0.97 for version-family construction and 0.94 for rule-level consistency, with CSP raising rule consistency from 0.90 to 0.94.

</details>


### [200] [NeMo Data Designer: An Extensible Framework for Multimodal Synthetic Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17699)
*Johnny Greco,Nabin Mulepati,Andre Manoel,Eric Tramel,Kirit Thadaka,Mike Knepper,Dhruv Nathawani,Dane Corneil,Yev Meyer,Alex Watson,Maarten Van Segbroeck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: NeMo Data Designer (NDD) 是一个开源、通用的多模态合成数据生成框架，采用声明式配置定义数据集列，内置预览-修订循环，支持插件扩展、依赖解析与请求重试，已在 Nemotron 模型研发和生产级企业部署中落地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态合成数据生成（SDG）在模型研发中需求广泛，但现有方案在易用性、可复现性、多模态覆盖与迭代式工作流支持方面存在不足，缺乏通用、可扩展且配置可审查的框架。

Method: NDD 以声明式配置格式描述数据集各列，列类型涵盖文本、代码、结构化输出、图像、嵌入及用于调控数据多样性的统计采样器；通过插件系统扩展列类型与功能。其核心工作流内建预览-修订循环（先生成少量记录、检查并精化规范，再全量生成）；运行时负责解析依赖、调度对用户提供的模型端点的调用并重试失败请求。配置本身是可审查产物，支持工作流共享与复现。

Result: 论文给出了 NDD 的架构与编程模型，并通过结构化、智能体（agentic）、多模态及领域专用任务的案例研究验证其能力，包括用于 Nemotron 模型开发的数据集与生产环境企业部署中的数据集。

Conclusion: NDD 提供了一个开源、通用、可复现且可扩展的多模态合成数据生成框架，其声明式配置与预览-修订范式兼顾易用性与规模化生成能力，并已在实际模型研发与企业生产中得到应用。

Abstract: We present NeMo Data Designer (NDD), an open-source, general-purpose framework for multi-modal synthetic data generation (SDG). Designed to be intuitive to use, NDD provides a declarative configuration format in which human and/or agent users define each dataset column, with column types spanning text, code, structured outputs, images, embeddings, and statistical samplers that are explicitly configured to steer dataset diversity. Additional column types and functionality can be introduced using the framework's flexible plugin system. NDD's configuration is an inspectable artifact, supporting workflow sharing and reproducibility. SDG is an inherently iterative process. NDD therefore builds a preview-and-revision loop into its core workflow, allowing users to generate and inspect a small number of records, refine the specification, and rerun generation at full scale. At runtime, NDD resolves dependencies, schedules calls to user-provided model endpoints, and retries failed requests. We describe NDD's architecture and programming model and present case studies spanning structured, agentic, multimodal, and domain-specialized tasks, including datasets used in Nemotron model development and in production enterprise deployments.

</details>


### [201] [A Systematic Evaluation of the COTQ Provincial Land Cover Product: Structural Consistency, Spectral Separability, and Relative Positioning Against ESA, ESRI, and Google Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17731)
*Étienne Clabaut,Samuel Foucher,Yacine Bouroubi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该文系统评估了魁北克省10米土地覆盖产品COTQ，与ESA WorldCover、ESRI LandCover和Google DynamicWorld三个全球10米产品对比，通过结构指标、光谱可分性和分歧区目视解译，在代表魁北克主要生物气候域的8个Sentinel-2瓦片上进行验证，未提出新制图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分辨率LULC产品在生态梯度复杂、地表异质性强的区域表现不一，魁北克为支持年度土地占用与土壤人工化监测而开发了省级10米产品COTQ，需客观刻画其相对定位。

Method: 不作新方法创新，聚焦COTQ行为与一致性分析；将四产品统一到公共图例；采用结构指标（对象尺寸分布、形状复杂度、调整兰德指数ARI、交并比IoU）、基于Sentinel-2反射率的光谱可分性度量，以及针对分歧区的定向目视解译；覆盖8个Sentinel-2瓦片，代表从温带、寒温带森林到北部苔原的主要生物气候域。

Result: COTQ的结构与光谱特征在三个全球参考产品中最接近ESA WorldCover；同时暴露出与类别定义和主题优先级相关的系统性差异，尤其体现在城市、湿地以及岩石或隐花植物地表。

Conclusion: 该多准则评估客观刻画了COTQ产品特征，明确了其相对现有全球土地覆盖数据集的定位，可为魁北克业务化土地监测提供参考。

Abstract: High-resolution land use and land cover (LULC) products derived from Sentinel-2 imagery are widely used for environmental monitoring and land management, yet their performance can vary across regions with complex ecological gradients and heterogeneous surface conditions. In Quebec, these limitations motivated the development of a provincial 10-m land-cover product, the COTQ, designed to support annual monitoring of land occupation and soil artificialisation. This study presents a systematic evaluation of the COTQ product relative to three global 10-m LULC datasets: ESA WorldCover, ESRI LandCover, and Google DynamicWorld. This paper does not introduce a new mapping methodology but focuses on analysing the behaviour and consistency of the COTQ using complementary evaluation approaches. All products are harmonized under a common legend and compared using structural indicators (object-size distributions, shape complexity, Adjusted Rand Index, and Intersection over Union), spectral separability metrics derived from Sentinel-2 reflectance data, and a targeted photo-interpretation of disagreement areas. The analysis is conducted over eight Sentinel-2 tiles selected to represent the main bioclimatic domains of Québec, from temperate and boreal forests to northern tundra environments. The results show that the COTQ exhibits structural and spectral characteristics most similar to ESA WorldCover among the reference global products, while revealing systematic differences linked to class definitions and thematic priorities, particularly for urban areas, wetlands, and rocky or cryptogamic surfaces. This multi-criteria evaluation provides an objective characterization of the COTQ product and clarifies its relative positioning with respect to existing global land-cover datasets for operational land monitoring in Québec.

</details>


### [202] [Imitation Learning for Autonomous Driving in CARLA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17757)
*Jordy Kieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究紧凑多模态策略在CARLA中仅用离线专家示范能获得多少闭环驾驶能力，并发布1.36M参数模型及配套资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆虽为离线训练，但部署是闭环的：当前动作会影响后续观测。论文旨在量化紧凑多模态策略仅凭离线示范在CARLA中可获得的闭环驾驶能力。

Method: 策略输入为RGB图像、LiDAR、车辆遥测与车道航点的五帧历史，输出油门、刹车与转向，频率20Hz。示范分三阶段采集，末期采用系统化路线生成流程（枚举生成点与可行机动并验证完成的自动驾驶路线）。模型1.36M参数，在236,882个窗口上训练，约对应448次采集的3.3小时驾驶数据。

Result: 策略在训练及留出路线上可自主驾驶数小时；在作者实验中未发生碰撞，并定性迁移至道路几何不同的未见CARLA城镇。另观察到大幅轨迹偏差后的恢复，但未声称系统性恢复（无受控评测）。论文报告离线指标，并区分实测结果与定性闭环观察。

Conclusion: 紧凑多模态行为克隆策略可从离线示范获得较强的闭环驾驶能力，并在未见城镇展现定性迁移与一定恢复能力；同时强调定性观察与受控评测的界限，并开源代码、检查点、ONNX模型、数据样例与证据审计。

Abstract: Behavioral cloning trains a policy offline on expert demonstrations, but deployment is closed loop: each action affects the observations the policy receives next. We study how much closed-loop driving competence a compact multimodal policy can acquire from offline demonstrations in the CARLA simulator. The policy uses five-frame histories of RGB images, LiDAR, vehicle telemetry, and lane waypoints to predict throttle, brake, and steering at 20 Hz. Demonstrations were collected in three stages, ending with a systematic route-generation procedure that enumerates spawn points and feasible maneuvers and verifies completed autopilot routes. The released 1.36 million parameter policy was trained on 236,882 windows, representing about 3.3 hours of driving from 448 captures. The resulting policy drives autonomously for hours on training and held-out routes. In our runs, it did so without collisions and also transferred qualitatively to an unseen CARLA town with different road geometry. We also observed recovery from large trajectory deviations, although we do not claim systematic recovery without controlled evaluation. We report offline metrics and distinguish measured results from qualitative closed-loop observations. We release the code, trained checkpoint, ONNX model, data sample, and an evidence audit for the reported claims.

</details>


### [203] [SAGE: Governed Artifact Generation from Enterprise Guidelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17775)
*Mohammadreza Sediqin,Shivali Dalmia,Sumukha Thoppanahalli,Srinivasa Karthikeya Reddy Kovvuri,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SAGE 是一个带治理机制的多阶段 LLM 流水线，将企业指南文档转化为结构化工作产物，把原本需 2-3 天的人工工作压缩到 20-100 分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业指南文档混合叙述文本、复杂表格和嵌入图像，人工转结构化产物需 2-3 天。现有语言与视觉语言模型仅做抽取，缺乏验证、一致性检查与可追溯的产物生成等治理工作流。

Method: SAGE 以共享的带版本规则库为核心，具有稳定标识符、经模式校验的跨阶段契约和端到端溯源追踪。抽取出的规则先经确定性结构验证与基于 LLM 的语义评分，再由一致性模块去重、标记矛盾并暴露规范缺口；仅不确定或被标记项交由人工审核，高置信输出自动批准。

Result: 在 120 份文档上，周转时间从数天降至 20-100 分钟，文档级成功率 96%，幻觉率 3.2%，抽取 3,896 条规则并生成 812 个可交付人工审核的产物；无治理时幻觉率升至 15.7%。

Conclusion: 治理机制是多阶段 LLM 抽取流水线的关键：在共享版本化规则库、契约校验与溯源追踪支撑下，SAGE 显著提升效率与可靠性，并将幻觉率大幅压低。

Abstract: Enterprise guideline documents mix narrative text, complex tables, and embedded images, and converting them into structured work artifacts still takes two to three days of manual effort each. Current language and vision-language models extract from such documents but offer no governed workflow beyond extraction: no validation, no consistency checking, no traceable artifact generation. We introduce SAGE, a governed multi-stage LLM pipeline organized around a shared versioned rule store with stable identifiers, schema-validated inter-stage contracts, and end-to-end provenance tracking. Extracted rules undergo deterministic structural validation and LLM-based semantic scoring, then a consistency module that removes duplicates, flags contradictions, and surfaces specification gaps; only uncertain or flagged items reach reviewers, while high-confidence outputs are auto-approved. On 120 documents, SAGE cuts turnaround from days to 20-100 minutes, achieving a 96% document-level success rate with 3.2% hallucination, extracting 3,896 rules and producing 812 artifacts ready for human review; without governance, hallucination rises to 15.7%.

</details>


### [204] [FairCompressAgent: An Agentic Framework for Fairness-Aware Model Compression for FPGA Deployment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17786)
*Yuanbo Guo,Yiyu Shi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 FairCompressAgent (FCA) 智能体框架，通过统一的算子接口整合公平性感知剪枝、增量量化与稀疏低秩分解，利用语言模型规划器结合模型画像与实测结果选择压缩配置，在 Fitzpatrick-17k 与 VGG-11 上以更少候选评估次数实现存储压缩、精度提升与公平性指标改善。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平性感知的模型压缩需要在精度、公平性与部署成本之间权衡选取方法与配置；当压缩方法被组合使用或用户需求发生变化时，这类决策更加困难。现有方法缺乏对组合压缩方案的统一接口、基于实测反馈的配置搜索以及对需求变更的交互式支持。

Method: 提出 FairCompressAgent (FCA)：1）通过统一的算子接口整合公平性感知剪枝、增量量化与稀疏低秩分解三类压缩方法；2）采用语言模型规划器，依据模型画像与实测结果选择压缩配置；3）执行层负责压缩、微调、评估与基于约束的筛选；4）支持需求更新，并在请求无法满足时报告残余违反量。在 Fitzpatrick-17k 数据集与 VGG-11 模型上，对 40 个实测配置比较四种搜索方法。

Result: 在精度约束请求下，FCA 选出的压缩模型推理张量存储减少 59.54%，同时验证集平均精度由 0.5141 提升至 0.5233，均等机会（EOpp）由 0.2251 降至 0.2168。在各自停止策略下，FCA 平均仅需 7.33 次候选评估即达到与一次性规划相同的最终选择，后者需 12 次。通过重复微调、留出测试与在线需求更新，验证了该压缩流程的稳定性与交互可用性。

Conclusion: 实验表明，基于实测反馈与显式约束的智能体框架能够有效支持公平性感知压缩配置的选择与交互式细化，在降低推理存储的同时兼顾甚至提升精度与公平性，并减少配置搜索所需的评估代价。

Abstract: Fairness-aware model compression requires selecting methods and configurations that balance accuracy, fairness, and deployment cost. These decisions become more difficult when compression methods are composed or the user's requirements change. In this paper, we propose FairCompressAgent (FCA), an agentic framework that integrates fairness-aware pruning, incremental quantization, and sparse low-rank factorization through a common operator interface. A language-model planner uses model profiles and measured outcomes to select compression configurations, while an execution layer performs compression, fine-tuning, evaluation, and constraint-based selection. FCA also supports requirement updates and reports the remaining violation when a request cannot be satisfied. Experiments on Fitzpatrick-17k with VGG-11 compare four search methods over 40 measured configurations. Under the accuracy-constrained request, FCA selects a compressed model with 59.54% less inference tensor storage, while validation average precision increases from 0.5141 to 0.5233 and equalized opportunity (EOpp) decreases from 0.2251 to 0.2168. It reaches the same final selection as one-shot planning with 7.33 versus 12 candidate evaluations on average, under their respective stopping policies. Repeated fine-tuning, held-out testing, and online requirement updates characterize the stability and interactive use of this compression workflow. The results demonstrate how measured feedback and explicit constraints support the selection and interactive refinement of fairness-aware compression configurations.

</details>


### [205] [A Four-Stage Decomposition of Word-Problem Solving and Mechanistic Fragility in LLM Math Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17804)
*Zhongdi Qu,Carla P. Gomes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对大语言模型解数学应用题的过程给出机制性解释：内部计算分解为四阶段顺序流水线（模式抽象、运算规划、操作数绑定、计算），各阶段在不同层带产生可识别的中间表征；干扰子导致的失败定位于运算规划这一单阶段，由一组注意力头实现，并经双向因果验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型解小学难度数学应用题准确率很高，但插入一个无关子句就会使其崩溃。作者希望统一解释"高准确率"与"易受干扰"这两个看似矛盾的现象，并给出机制层面的说明。

Method: 机制可解释性分析。将模型内部计算刻画为四阶段顺序流水线：模式抽象、运算规划、操作数绑定、计算，每阶段在可识别的层带产生独立的中间表征。复用同一脚手架诊断干扰诱发的失败，定位受损阶段为运算规划，并识别实现该阶段的注意力头，通过双向干预验证其因果作用。

Result: 模型推理可分解为四阶段流水线，各阶段对应可识别的层带与中间表征；干扰导致的失败被定位到单一阶段——运算规划；该阶段由一组特定注意力头实现，双向因果验证支持其因果角色。

Conclusion: 该研究为LLM的数学应用题推理及受干扰时的失败提供了机制性解释，把行为层面的脆弱性归结为流水线中特定阶段（运算规划）及其注意力头的问题，为理解与改进模型的鲁棒性提供了可操作的定位依据。

Abstract: Large language models solve grade-school math word problems with high accuracy, yet a single irrelevant clause inserted into the problem can collapse it. We reconcile these observations with a mechanistic account. We show that the model's internal computation decomposes into a four-stage sequential pipeline, Schema Abstraction, Operation Planning, Operand Binding, and Computation, each stage producing a distinct intermediate representation in an identifiable band of layers. Using the same scaffold to diagnose distractor-induced failure, we localize the corruption to a single stage, Operation Planning, implemented by a set of attention heads whose causal role we validate bidirectionally. In short, we provide a mechanistic interpretation of math word problem reasoning in LLMs, and their failure when distracted.

</details>


### [206] [Learning Heterogeneous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17847)
*Shiwali Mohan,Matt Hong,Dule Shu,Aniek Fransen,Shabnam Hakimi,Matt Klenk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究主观偏好学习问题，提出以个体和决策情境为条件的"个体化效用函数"，并设计多阶段架构从多模态数据估计该函数；在57.5万+配对美学判断数据集上，个体化效用模型显著优于通用效用模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从人类反馈中学习的方法通常假设存在一个跨人群共享的"通用效用函数"，并将标注者之间的分歧视为随机噪声。该假设适用于客观任务，但在主观领域中失效——因为偏好会系统性地随个体差异而变化。作者希望证明分歧反映的是有意义的偏好异质性，而非标注噪声。

Method: 借鉴理性选择理论（RCT），引入同时以个体及其决策情境为条件的"个体化效用函数（individuated utility functions）"，并提出一种新颖的多阶段架构，从多模态数据中估计这些效用函数。数据方面，新收集了来自2398名参与者、超过575,000条关于汽车轮毂设计比较的成对美学判断数据集。

Result: 实验表明，个体化效用模型大幅优于通用效用模型，包括各种基础模型（foundation model）基线。结果说明标注者间的分歧体现的是有意义的偏好异质性，而非标注噪声。

Conclusion: 研究强调了收集标注者属性信息、学习个体化效用函数的重要性，从而构建能够明确说明"代表谁的偏好"并能忠实捕捉人类决策多样性的奖励模型。

Abstract: Learning from human feedback has become a central paradigm for training modern AI systems, where models of human utility are used as reward models in policy learning. Existing methods typically assume a \emph{universal utility} function shared across a population and treat disagreement between annotators as stochastic variation. While suitable for objective tasks, this assumption breaks down in subjective domains where preferences vary systematically across individuals. We study the problem of subjective preference learning, in which observed choices arise from heterogeneous but internally consistent utility functions. Drawing upon rational choice theory, RCT \parencite{tversky1981framing}, we introduce \emph{individuated utility} functions conditioned on both the individual and their decision context, and propose a novel multi-stage architecture for estimating them from multi-modal data. We evaluate our framework on a newly collected dataset of more than $575{,}000$ pairwise aesthetic judgments from $2{,}398$ participants comparing automotive wheel designs. Our experiments show that individuated utility models substantially outperform universal utility models including foundation model baselines. Our results demonstrate that disagreement reflects meaningful preference heterogeneity rather than annotation noise. More broadly, our findings highlight the importance of collecting annotator attributes and learning individuated utility functions, enabling reward models that explicitly account for whose preferences they represent and faithfully capture human decision diversity.

</details>


### [207] [SNOMED CT Concept Recommendation from Masked Clinical Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17855)
*Ali Noori*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于SNOMED CT实体链接挑战赛数据的掩码概念推荐基准，比较多种推荐方法。稀疏TF-IDF表现最佳（Recall@1 14.81%，Recall@10 33.43%，MRR 0.2114，nDCG@10 0.2297），检索增强未能提升基线。性能受概念频率强烈影响，低频概念Recall@10仅7.74%，9.66%测试对含训练未见概念，表明局部词汇上下文与术语覆盖度是低资源推荐质量的关键决定因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将临床语言标准化到SNOMED CT可支持互操作性、分析与可复用表型构建，但当相关概念在训练数据中稀有或缺失时，概念推荐仍然困难，因此需要建立低资源场景下的可复现基准。

Method: 使用SNOMED CT Entity Linking Challenge v1.2.1（源自MIMIC-IV-Note）构建掩码概念推荐基准：75,491条标注、272份出院小结，其中204份训练、68份历史测试。对每个唯一的笔记-概念对，从局部临床上下文中掩蔽目标提及，系统对训练中观测到的SNOMED CT概念进行排序。比较流行度基线、稀疏TF-IDF概念原型、稠密LSA嵌入、稀疏-稠密融合、检索笔记证据及检索增强混合方法。

Result: 稀疏TF-IDF最佳：Recall@1 14.81%、Recall@10 33.43%、MRR 0.2114、nDCG@10 0.2297；检索增强未改善基线（Recall@10 31.99%、MRR 0.1937）。性能受概念频率强烈影响：仅出现在1-2份训练笔记中的概念Recall@10为7.74%，而出现超过10份的为43.90%；此外9.66%的测试笔记-概念对包含训练未见概念。

Conclusion: 局部词汇上下文与术语覆盖度是低资源环境下推荐质量的主要决定因素，研究提供了面向未来本体驱动与生物医学编码器检索系统的可复现基线。

Abstract: Standardizing clinical language to SNOMED CT supports interoperability, analytics, and reusable phenotyping, but concept recommendation remains difficult when relevant concepts are rare or absent from training data. We present a masked-concept recommendation benchmark using the SNOMED CT Entity Linking Challenge v1.2.1 data derived from MIMIC-IV-Note. The dataset contains 75,491 annotations across 272 discharge summaries, with 204 notes used for training and 68 for historical testing. For each unique note-concept pair, the target mention is masked from a local clinical context and the system ranks SNOMED CT concepts observed during training. We compare a popularity baseline, sparse TF-IDF concept prototypes, dense latent semantic analysis embeddings, sparse-dense fusion, retrieved-note evidence, and a retrieval-augmented hybrid. Sparse TF-IDF performs best, achieving Recall@1 of 14.81%, Recall@10 of 33.43%, MRR of 0.2114, and nDCG@10 of 0.2297. Retrieval augmentation does not improve this baseline, with Recall@10 of 31.99% and MRR of 0.1937. Performance is strongly affected by concept frequency: Recall@10 is 7.74% for concepts appearing in only one or two training notes versus 43.90% for concepts appearing in more than ten. In addition, 9.66% of test note-concept pairs contain concepts unseen during training. These findings show that local lexical context and terminology coverage are major determinants of recommendation quality in low-resource settings and provide a reproducible baseline for future ontology-grounded and biomedical-encoder retrieval systems.

</details>


### [208] [The Inference Engineering Pareto Atlas: Which Optimizations Dominate the Cost, Quality, and Latency Frontier?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17863)
*Srikanta Datta Tumkur,Jay Iyer,Mehar Simhadri,Sai Pavan Kumar,Sai Kapil Kumar,Ramesh Nampelly*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文构建成本-质量-延迟帕累托图谱，以54个配置校准模拟器，系统比较LLM推理优化方法的部署取舍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM推理优化报告在模型、GPU、提示和评测指标上不统一，导致加速效果难以横向比较或组合使用。

Method: 在vLLM 0.12上于L4/A100/H100测量Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct的54个配置作为锚点校准模拟器；对FP16、AWQ 4bit、FP8权重、FP8 KV cache做200题GSM8K质量评测；稀疏注意力仅模拟评估。

Result: 模拟器复现误差低于1.5%；36个配置中18个进入帕累托前沿，组合方法命中率更高（9/15对9/21）。AWQ 4bit延迟降至0.34倍但严格准确率损失5.9%；FP8权重保留99.4%准确率、延迟0.61–0.65倍并进入四个区域赢家中的三个；FP8 KV cache无正确输出；n-gram推测解码无收益。

Conclusion: 最优配置取决于约束与GPU：H100适合紧延迟，A100适合吞吐与低成本（0.106美元/百万token），不能仅以速度作为选择标准。

Abstract: LLM inference optimizations report speedups on different models, GPUs, prompts, and quality metrics, making them hard to compare or combine. We build a cost, quality, and latency Pareto atlas to identify the best configurations for different deployment constraints.
